📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • H5TX Q226L 突变体结合人型受体,但需要额外的 突变 来结合人呼吸道组织,并且 H5FR Q226L 中的多碱基切割位点改变了受体结合特异性。 [10.1128/jvi.01065-26]。
  • 疫苗接种 与基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 储备 疫苗 在两种动物模型中诱导强烈的中和抗体反应,显着抑制病毒复制,并提供针对致命挑战的完全保护。相比之下,所有未接种疫苗的小鼠和雪貂均死于感染。
  • A(H5N5) 在人支气管上皮细胞中有效复制,并在所有接种的雪貂中引起致命的全身性疾病,4 天内在直接接触的动物中检测到有限的 传播,并且没有观察到空气传播的 传播。 [10.1128/jvi.00856-26]。
  • 在1,591个H9N2病毒中总共鉴定出52个基因型,其中包括48个新定义的基因型(G118-G163),其中G57和G118成为主要流行基因型。 [10.1080/22221751.2026.2713320]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The H5TX Q226L mutant bound human-type receptors but required additional mutations to bind human respiratory tract tissue, and the multibasic cleavage site in H5FR Q226L altered receptor-binding specificity.
  • Vaccination with the AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based stockpiled vaccine induced robust neutralising antibody responses in both animal models, significantly suppressed viral replication, and conferred complete protection against lethal challenge.
  • A(H5N5) replicated efficiently in human bronchial epithelial cells and caused fatal systemic disease in all inoculated ferrets, with limited transmission detected in direct contact animals within 4 days and no airborne transmission observed.
  • A total of 52 genotypes were identified among 1,591 H9N2 viruses, including 48 newly defined genotypes (G118–G163), with G57 and G118 emerging as the dominant epidemic genotypes. [10.1080/22221751.2026.2713320].

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 新南威尔士州第一产业和区域发展部 (NSW DPIRD) 与澳大利亚政府合作报告称,新南威尔士州三个地点的四个 野生鸟类 已确认检测到 H5 禽流感 (H5N1)。该事件已得到证实并正在调查中。
  • 能源、环境和气候行动部 (DEECA) 已确认凯西市发现一例 H5 禽流感病例;由凯西市作为官方议会新闻报道;事件已确认。 [news-4f571d7ea7fc82d9]。
  • H5N1 高致病性禽流感 已进入澳大利亚,自 2026 年 6 月起在野生海鸟中得到证实,有记录表明对标志性和濒危本土动物存在风险,并且未来可能传播到家禽;澳大利亚政府正在启动针对圈养濒危物种的 疫苗接种 行动。
  • 南澳大利亚内陆地区发现了禽流感(H5N1),距离罗布100多公里处报告了新病例,罗布一直是澳大利亚禽流感的焦点地区暴发。野生动物专家和鸟类饲养者呼吁 疫苗 保护濒危物种和私人鸟类。 [news-e98445524df913f9]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Avian influenza A viruses news developments.
  • Confirmed detection of H5 bird flu (H5N1) in four wild birds across three NSW locations, reported by the NSW Department of Primary Industries and Regional Development (NSW DPIRD) in partnership with the Australian Government.
  • The Department of Energy, Environment and Climate Action (DEECA) has confirmed that the City of Casey has recorded a case of H5 bird flu; reported by the City of Casey as official council news; event is confirmed.
  • H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza has entered Australia, confirmed in wild seabirds since June 2026, with documented risk to iconic and endangered native fauna and potential future spread to poultry; Australian government is initiating targeted vaccination of captive endangered species.
  • Bird flu (H5N1) has been detected inland in South Australia, with reports of new cases more than 100 kilometres from Robe, which has been a focal point of Australia's bird flu outbreak.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: EBioMedicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-11  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ebiom.2026.106430  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 131, 106430
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

A(H5N8) 储存的 疫苗 对与人类分离的牛 A(H5N1) 流感病毒基因相同的病毒的保护作用。

Protective effect of A(H5N8) stockpiled vaccine against a virus genetically identical to a human isolate of bovine A(H5N1) influenza virus.
作者： Ryuta Uraki, Maki Kiso, Kiyoko Iwatsuki-Horimoto, Mutsumi Ito, Seiya Yamayoshi, Masaki Imai, Yoshihiro Kawaoka, Kiyoko Iwatsuki‐Horimoto
摘要中文翻译背景:自 2024 年初以来,进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 病毒已在美国奶牛中引起广泛的 暴发 感染,跨物种溢出 感染包括人类在内的哺乳动物物种。从人类病例中分离出的一种牛源性 A(H5N1) 病毒在哺乳动物模型中保留了高致病性和传播性,凸显了其大流行的潜力。大流行前储存的流感 疫苗 旨在在毒株匹配的 疫苗 可用之前提供早期保护;然而,它们对牛 A(H5N1) 病毒的保护功效尚未在体内直接评估。方法:在本研究中,我们使用小鼠和雪貂模型评估了日本储存的基于 AS03 佐剂的 A/Astrakhan/3212/2020 (H5N8) 分支 2.3.4.4b 流感 疫苗 的保护功效。接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的动物接受了与人类分离的牛 A(H5N1) 病毒基因相同的病毒的攻击。评估了中和抗体反应、器官中的病毒复制和存活率。结果:疫苗接种 与基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 储备 疫苗 在两种动物模型中诱导了强烈的中和抗体反应,显着抑制病毒复制,并提供针对致命攻击的完全保护。相比之下,所有未接种疫苗的小鼠和雪貂均死于感染。这些发现表明,基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 储备 疫苗 对牛 A(H5N1) 病毒具有强大的交叉保护功效。解读:日本储存的基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 疫苗 可以在大流行的早期阶段作为针对牛 A(H5N1) 病毒的直接对策。资助:这项工作得到了日本传染病研究和基础设施计划 (JP20wm0125002) 和日本医学研究与发展机构的日本世界领先 疫苗 研究与开发中心倡议 (JP223fa627001) 的资助。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 2024 年初以来,分支 2.3.4.4b 的 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 病毒已在美国奶牛中引起广泛的 暴发,跨物种溢出 也感染到包括人类在内的哺乳动物物种。从人类病例中分离出的一种牛源性 A(H5N1) 病毒在哺乳动物模型中保留了高致病性和传播性,凸显了其大流行的潜力。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们使用小鼠和雪貂模型评估了日本储存的基于 AS03 佐剂的 A/Astrakhan/3212/2020 (H5N8) 分支 2.3.4.4b 流感 疫苗 的保护功效。接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的动物接受了与人类分离的牛 A(H5N1) 病毒基因相同的病毒的攻击。
核心方法
该研究评估了中和抗体反应、器官中的病毒复制以及接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的动物模型的存活率。基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 储备 疫苗 已针对与牛 A(H5N1) 病毒的人类分离株基因相同的病毒进行了测试。
主要结果
疫苗接种 与基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 储备 疫苗 在两种动物模型中诱导强烈的中和抗体反应,显着抑制病毒复制,并提供针对致命挑战的完全保护。相比之下,所有未接种疫苗的小鼠和雪貂均死于感染。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 储备 疫苗 对牛 A(H5N1) 病毒具有强大的交叉保护功效。日本库存的基于 AS03 佐剂的 A(H5N8) 疫苗 可以在大流行的早期阶段作为针对牛 A(H5N1) 病毒的直接对策。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,储存的基于 A(H5N8) 的 疫苗 可以提供针对相关牛 A(H5N1) 病毒的保护,这可以为大流行防范策略提供信息。如果发生涉及 A(H5N1) 病毒的人畜共患 暴发,这可能会对大流行前 疫苗 的使用产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在动物模型中进行的,可能无法完全反映人类 免疫应答。证据充分证明 疫苗 对基因相同的牛 A(H5N1) 病毒具有保护功效,但对人类群体的普遍适用性仍不确定。

Protective effect of A(H5N8) stockpiled vaccine against a virus genetically identical to a human isolate of bovine A(H5N1) influenza virus.

Authors: Ryuta Uraki, Maki Kiso, Kiyoko Iwatsuki-Horimoto, Mutsumi Ito, Seiya Yamayoshi, Masaki Imai, Yoshihiro Kawaoka, Kiyoko Iwatsuki‐Horimoto
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Since early 2024, highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b have caused extensive outbreaks in dairy cattle in the United States, with spillover into mammalian species, including humans. A bovine-derived A(H5N1) virus isolated from a human case retains high pathogenicity and transmissibility in mammalian models, highlighting its pandemic potential. Stockpiled pre-pandemic influenza vaccines are intended to provide early protection before strain-matched vaccines are available; however, their protective efficacy against bovine A(H5N1) viruses has not been directly evaluated in vivo. METHODS: In this study, we assessed the protective efficacy of an AS03-adjuvanted A/Astrakhan/3212/2020 (H5N8) clade 2.3.4.4b-based influenza vaccine stockpiled in Japan using mouse and ferret models. Vaccinated and unvaccinated animals were challenged with a virus genetically identical to a human isolate of bovine A(H5N1) virus. Neutralising antibody responses, viral replication in organs, and survival were evaluated. FINDINGS: Vaccination with the AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based stockpiled vaccine induced robust neutralising antibody responses in both animal models, significantly suppressed viral replication, and conferred complete protection against lethal challenge. In contrast, all unvaccinated mice and ferrets succumbed to infection. These findings demonstrate that the AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based stockpiled vaccine provides strong cross-protective efficacy against bovine A(H5N1) viruses. INTERPRETATION: An AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based vaccine stockpiled in Japan could serve as an immediate countermeasure against bovine A(H5N1) viruses during the early phase of a pandemic. FUNDING: This work was supported by grants from the Japan Program for Infectious Diseases Research and Infrastructure (JP20wm0125002) and the Japan Initiative for World-leading Vaccine Research and Development Centers (JP223fa627001) from the Japan Agency for Medical Research and Development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Since early 2024, highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) viruses of clade 2.3.4.4b have caused extensive outbreaks in dairy cattle in the United States, with spillover into mammalian species, including humans. A bovine-derived A(H5N1) virus isolated from a human case retains high pathogenicity and transmissibility in mammalian models, highlighting its pandemic potential.
Design and population
In this study, we assessed the protective efficacy of an AS03-adjuvanted A/Astrakhan/3212/2020 (H5N8) clade 2.3.4.4b-based influenza vaccine stockpiled in Japan using mouse and ferret models. Vaccinated and unvaccinated animals were challenged with a virus genetically identical to a human isolate of bovine A(H5N1) virus.
Core methods
The study evaluated neutralising antibody responses, viral replication in organs, and survival in both vaccinated and unvaccinated animal models. The AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based stockpiled vaccine was tested against a virus genetically identical to a human isolate of bovine A(H5N1) virus.
Main results
Vaccination with the AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based stockpiled vaccine induced robust neutralising antibody responses in both animal models, significantly suppressed viral replication, and conferred complete protection against lethal challenge. In contrast, all unvaccinated mice and ferrets succumbed to infection.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate that the AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based stockpiled vaccine provides strong cross-protective efficacy against bovine A(H5N1) viruses. An AS03-adjuvanted A(H5N8)-based vaccine stockpiled in Japan could serve as an immediate countermeasure against bovine A(H5N1) viruses during the early phase of a pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that stockpiled A(H5N8)-based vaccines may offer protection against related bovine A(H5N1) viruses, which could inform pandemic preparedness strategies. This could have implications for the use of pre-pandemic vaccines in the event of a zoonotic outbreak involving A(H5N1) viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in animal models, which may not fully reflect human immune responses. The evidence is strong for the protective efficacy of the vaccine against genetically identical bovine A(H5N1) viruses, but generalisability to human populations remains uncertain.

Journal: Journal of virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-10  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.01065-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0106526
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

2.3.4.4b H5N1 血凝素获得特定的人呼吸道结合需要多个 突变。

Acquisition of specific human respiratory tract binding by 2.3.4.4b H5N1 hemagglutinins requires multiple mutations.
作者： María Ríos Carrasco, Mafalda F. Guerreiro Cabana, Eszter Kovács, Zoé Ducarne, Cindy G. J. Cleypool, Geert-Jan Boons, Robert P. de Vries, Eszter Kovacs
摘要中文翻译摘要 有人提出,感染人类的​​牛源 2.3.4.4b 病毒 A/Texas/34 H5N1 (H5TX) 的血凝素仅需要一个 突变,即 Q226L,即可从结合禽类受体偏好转变为结合人型受体偏好。在这项研究中,我们检查了 H5TX Q226L 以及其他关键 突变 与人类气管切片的结合。我们的结论是,虽然 H5TX Q226L 可以结合人型受体,但该蛋白需要多个 突变 才能与人呼吸道组织结合。我们还报告了另一种 2.3.4.4b HA 突变体 H5FR Q226L(来自 A/duck/France/161108/16 H5N8)的受体结合特异性的变化,该变化与多碱基切割位点的存在相关。这项研究深入了解了 进化 在当前流行的 H5Nx 病毒中与人型受体结合的决定因素。它还强调使用其他方法(包括基于组织的方法以及合成聚糖)测试单个菌株的重要性。重要性 目前,H5N1 甲型流感病毒导致许多人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件,从感染鸟类到其他哺乳动物,包括奶牛。尽管尚未观察到人传人的 传播,但已有多人被感染。这种 宿主 范围的扩展通常与病毒表面蛋白之一血凝素的变化有关,血凝素可以将其偏好从鸟类型受体转变为人类型受体。为了更好地了解当前流行的 H5N1 病毒在人类之间传播的潜力,我们评估了 Q226L 突变 与其他氨基酸取代相结合对与人类气管结合的影响。我们还研究了多碱基切割位点(高致病性流感病毒株中存在的特定基序)对受体结合特性的影响。这些发现提供了对受体结合在流感感染中的作用的深入了解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了牛源性 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒 (A/Texas/34) 血凝素中的单个 Q226L 突变 是否足以将受体结合从鸟类型转变为人类型,这是哺乳动物适应和潜在人类 传播 的关键决定因素。
设计与对象
该研究使用人类气管组织切片来评估受体结合,检查了 2.3.4.4b H5N1 和 H5N8 病毒的血凝素变体,包括 Q226L 突变 和其他替换。
核心方法
评估了 H5TX Q226L 和其他血凝素突变体(包括多碱基切割位点变体 H5FR Q226L)与人气管切片的结合,以及合成聚糖以确定受体特异性变化。
主要结果
H5TX Q226L 突变体结合人型受体,但需要额外的 突变 来结合人呼吸道组织,并且 H5FR Q226L 中的多碱基切割位点改变了受体结合特异性。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,2.3.4.4b H5N1 中的受体与人类呼吸道组织的结合需要多个 突变,这凸显了哺乳动物适应 Q226L 之外的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要使用基于组织的检测和合成聚糖来测试单个菌株以评估大流行的可能性,从而为 监测 策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其侧重于体外结合测定和组织切片而受到限制,可能无法完全捕获体内 传播 动态,因此对证据的可信度不高。

Acquisition of specific human respiratory tract binding by 2.3.4.4b H5N1 hemagglutinins requires multiple mutations.

Authors: María Ríos Carrasco, Mafalda F. Guerreiro Cabana, Eszter Kovács, Zoé Ducarne, Cindy G. J. Cleypool, Geert-Jan Boons, Robert P. de Vries, Eszter Kovacs
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT It has been suggested that the hemagglutinin of the human-infecting cattle-derived 2.3.4.4b virus A/Texas/34 H5N1 (H5TX) requires only one mutation, namely Q226L, to switch from binding avian-type to human-type receptor preference. In this study, we examined the binding of H5TX Q226L, along with other key mutations, to sections of human trachea. We conclude that, while H5TX Q226L can bind human-type receptors, more than a single mutation is required for this protein to bind to human respiratory tract tissue. We also report changes in receptor-binding specificity of another 2.3.4.4b HA mutant, H5FR Q226L (from A/duck/France/161108/16 H5N8), associated with the presence of a multibasic cleavage site. This study offers insight into the determinants of evolution toward human-type receptor binding in currently circulating H5Nx viruses. It also emphasizes the importance of testing individual strains using additional methods, including tissue-based approaches, alongside synthetic glycans. IMPORTANCE Currently, H5N1 influenza A viruses are responsible for numerous zoonotic spillover events, from infecting birds to other mammals, including dairy cattle. Although no human-to-human transmission has been observed, several people have been infected. This host range expansion is typically linked to changes in one of the viral surface proteins, hemagglutinin, which can switch its preference from avian-type to human-type receptors. To better understand the potential of the currently circulating H5N1 virus to transmit among humans, we evaluated the effects of the Q226L mutation, in combination with other amino acid substitutions, on binding to the human trachea. We also studied the effect of the multibasic cleavage site, a specific motif present in highly pathogenic influenza strains, on receptor-binding properties. These findings provide insight into the role of receptor binding in influenza infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether the single Q226L mutation in the hemagglutinin of the cattle-derived 2.3.4.4b H5N1 virus (A/Texas/34) is sufficient to switch receptor binding from avian-type to human-type, a key determinant of mammalian adaptation and potential human transmission.
Design and population
The study examined hemagglutinin variants from 2.3.4.4b H5N1 and H5N8 viruses, including the Q226L mutation and other substitutions, using human trachea tissue sections to assess receptor binding.
Core methods
Binding to human trachea sections was evaluated for H5TX Q226L and other hemagglutinin mutants, including the multibasic cleavage site variant H5FR Q226L, alongside synthetic glycans to determine receptor specificity changes.
Main results
The H5TX Q226L mutant bound human-type receptors but required additional mutations to bind human respiratory tract tissue, and the multibasic cleavage site in H5FR Q226L altered receptor-binding specificity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that receptor binding to human respiratory tract tissue in 2.3.4.4b H5N1 requires more than a single mutation, highlighting the complexity of mammalian adaptation beyond Q226L.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform surveillance strategies by emphasizing the need to test individual strains using tissue-based assays alongside synthetic glycans to assess pandemic potential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on in vitro binding assays and tissue sections, which may not fully capture in vivo transmission dynamics, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

使用雪貂模型对首例人类病例中的 禽流感 A(H5N5) 病毒进行风险评估

Risk assessment of avian influenza A(H5N5) virus from the first human case using the ferret model
作者： Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza, Jessica A. Belser, Nicole Brock, Troy J. Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Xiangjie Sun, Juan A. De, La Cruz, Yasuko Hatta
摘要中文翻译摘要 欧亚起源的基因型 A6 A(H5N5) 病毒入侵北美,扩大了北美 高致病性禽流感 病毒的遗传多样性,并加剧了人们对人畜共患病风险的担忧。在人类致命感染 A(H5N5) 病毒 A/Washington/2148/2025 后,在极化的人支气管上皮细胞中评估了病毒复制,并在雪貂中评估了直接接触和呼吸道飞沫模型中的致病性、传播性以及空气传播的病毒脱落,为大流行风险评估提供信息。A(H5N5) 在 33°C 和 37°C 的 Calu-3 细胞中表现出强劲的复制能力,其动力学和峰滴度与当代基因型 B3.13 和 D1.1 A(H5N1) 病毒相当。在雪貂中,A(H5N5) 在呼吸道中有效复制,传播到肺外组织,并在所有接种的动物中引起致命疾病。没有观察到空气传播的 传播,并且空气样本中很少检测到低水平的病毒,与雪貂体内不通过空气传播的 A(H5) 病毒相似。在直接接触模型中,在接触后 4 天内检测到有限的 传播,有证据表明接触动物的下呼吸道复制。这些发现表明,A(H5N5)病毒具有在气道上皮细胞系中强大复制的能力,并且可以在雪貂中引起严重的全身感染和死亡率,但尚未获得在哺乳动物中适应空气传播的适应性。总的来说,这些结果强调了北美分支 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) 病毒之间的异质性,以及需要对新出现的病毒进行逐个基因型评估以了解公共卫生风险。重要性 欧亚起源的基因型 A6 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N5) 病毒在北美的出现增加了病毒多样性,并引发了对人畜共患和大流行风险的担忧。在本研究中,我们使用极化人支气管上皮细胞和雪貂模型评估了 A/Washington/2148/2025 A(H5N5) 病毒的复制动力学、发病机制 和 传播,该病毒是从首次报告的人类感染该流感病毒亚型中分离出来的。A(H5N5) 病毒在代表上呼吸道和下呼吸道的温度下在体外有效复制,并在接种的雪貂中引起致命的全身性疾病。在 4 天的直接接触期间观察到有限的 传播。空气传播病毒的检测很少,并且不会导致空气传播 传播。这些发现表明,A(H5N5) 病毒可以在哺乳动物细胞中强劲复制并引起严重疾病,但缺乏支持有效空气传播的适应性,这为评估基因型 A6 流感病毒造成的大流行风险提供了信息。在 4 天的直接接触期间观察到有限的 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
北美出现的欧亚起源基因型 A6 A(H5N5) 病毒增加了 高致病性禽流感 病毒的病毒多样性,并引起了对人畜共患和大流行风险的担忧,促使开展针对基因型的风险评估。
设计与对象
在人类感染 A(H5N5) 病毒 A/Washington/2148/2025 后,该研究评估了极化的人支气管上皮细胞中的病毒复制,并评估了雪貂中的致病性、传播性和空气传播的病毒脱落情况。
核心方法
在 33°C 和 37°C 的 Calu-3 细胞中测量病毒复制,同时接种雪貂以通过直接接触和呼吸道飞沫模型评估呼吸道中的复制、肺外传播、疾病严重程度和 传播。
主要结果
A(H5N5) 在人支气管上皮细胞中有效复制,并在所有接种的雪貂中引起致命的全身性疾病,4 天内在直接接触的动物中检测到有限的 传播,并且没有观察到空气传播的 传播。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,A(H5N5) 可以在哺乳动物细胞中强劲复制并引起严重疾病,但缺乏支持在哺乳动物中有效空气传播的适应性,突显了 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) 病毒之间的基因型特异性异质性。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,基因型 A6 A(H5N5) 具有严重的疾病潜力,但目前缺乏有效的哺乳动物 传播,从而为大流行风险评估提供信息,这强调了对新出现的病毒进行逐个基因型评估的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对雪貂模型的依赖和 传播 的观察期较短而受到限制,这可能无法完全捕捉与人类相关的 传播 动态或长期风险,因此需要谨慎地将证据强度解释为中等。

Risk assessment of avian influenza A(H5N5) virus from the first human case using the ferret model

Authors: Joanna A. Pulit-Penaloza, Jessica A. Belser, Nicole Brock, Troy J. Kieran, Claudia Pappas, Hui Zeng, Xiangjie Sun, Juan A. De, La Cruz, Yasuko Hatta
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The incursion of Eurasian-origin genotype A6 A(H5N5) virus into North America expanded the genetic diversity among North American highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses and heightened concern about zoonotic risk. Following a fatal human infection with the A(H5N5) virus A/Washington/2148/2025, viral replication was assessed in polarized human bronchial epithelial cells, and pathogenicity, transmissibility in direct contact and respiratory droplet models, and airborne virus shedding were evaluated in ferrets to inform pandemic risk assessment. A(H5N5) displayed robust replication in Calu-3 cells at 33°C and 37°C, showing kinetics and peak titers comparable to those of contemporary genotype B3.13 and D1.1 A(H5N1) viruses. In ferrets, A(H5N5) replicated efficiently in the respiratory tract, disseminated to extrapulmonary tissues, and caused fatal disease in all inoculated animals. Airborne transmission was not observed, and infrequent, low-level detection of virus in air samples paralleled that of A(H5) viruses that are not transmissible via air in ferrets. In a direct contact model, limited transmission was detected within 4 days of exposure, with evidence of lower respiratory tract replication in contact animals. These findings indicate that the A(H5N5) virus has the capacity for robust replication in an airway epithelial cell line and can cause severe systemic infection and mortality in ferrets but has not acquired adaptations for airborne spread in mammals. Collectively, these results underscore heterogeneity among clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) viruses in North America and the need for genotype-by-genotype evaluation of newly emerged viruses to understand public health risk. IMPORTANCE The emergence of Eurasian-origin genotype A6 highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N5) virus in North America has increased viral diversity and raised concerns about zoonotic and pandemic risk. In this study, we evaluated the replication kinetics, pathogenesis, and transmission of A/Washington/2148/2025 A(H5N5) virus, which was isolated from the first reported human infection with this influenza virus subtype, using polarized human bronchial epithelial cells and the ferret model. The A(H5N5) virus replicated efficiently in vitro at temperatures representative of the upper and lower respiratory tracts and caused fatal systemic disease in inoculated ferrets. Limited transmission was observed during 4 days of direct contact. Airborne virus detection was infrequent and did not result in airborne transmission. These findings show that A(H5N5) virus can replicate robustly in mammalian cells and cause severe disease but lacks adaptations supporting efficient airborne spread, informing assessment of the pandemic risk posed by genotype A6 influenza viruses. Limited transmission was observed during 4 days of direct contact.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The emergence of Eurasian-origin genotype A6 A(H5N5) virus in North America increased viral diversity among highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses and raised concerns about zoonotic and pandemic risk, prompting a genotype-specific risk assessment.
Design and population
Following a fatal human infection with A(H5N5) virus A/Washington/2148/2025, the study assessed viral replication in polarized human bronchial epithelial cells and evaluated pathogenicity, transmissibility, and airborne virus shedding in ferrets.
Core methods
Viral replication was measured in Calu-3 cells at 33°C and 37°C, while ferrets were inoculated to assess replication in the respiratory tract, extrapulmonary dissemination, disease severity, and transmission via direct contact and respiratory droplet models.
Main results
A(H5N5) replicated efficiently in human bronchial epithelial cells and caused fatal systemic disease in all inoculated ferrets, with limited transmission detected in direct contact animals within 4 days and no airborne transmission observed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that A(H5N5) can replicate robustly in mammalian cells and cause severe disease but lacks adaptations supporting efficient airborne spread in mammals, highlighting genotype-specific heterogeneity among clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform pandemic risk assessment by demonstrating that genotype A6 A(H5N5) poses severe disease potential but currently lacks efficient mammalian transmission, underscoring the need for genotype-by-genotype evaluation of newly emerged viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on the ferret model and short observation period for transmission, which may not fully capture human-relevant transmission dynamics or long-term risks, warranting cautious interpretation of the evidence strength as moderate.

首次检测到 禽流感 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 从南大洋入侵澳大利亚大陆

First detected incursions of avian influenza H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b into mainland Australia from the Southern Ocean
作者： Matthew J. Neave, Sam Hair, Patrick Mileto, Jackie E. Mahar, Vittoria Stevens, Kelly Davies, Mark O’Dea, Sadia Iqbal, Jamie W. L. Ong, Andrew Hughes
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性 禽流感 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 已引起对全球家禽和野生动物造成毁灭性影响的大流行。直到最近,澳大利亚大陆和更广阔的大洋洲仍然是最后一个没有确认检测到的主要地区。在此,我们报告了 2026 年 6 月在西澳大利亚南海岸发现的两种活海鸟(棕色贼鸥和南方巨海燕)中首次检测到的 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b。病毒 基因组 测序表明,这两种病毒与最近在南印度洋亚南极岛屿上检测到的 H5N1 病毒关系最密切。在时间校准的系统发育地理学分析中,在西澳大利亚采样的两种病毒都与来自澳大利亚亚南极外部领土赫德岛的病毒聚集在一起。祖先位置重建也将赫德岛确定为最可能的源位置,尽管不能排除未采样的中间位置。西澳大利亚州的两次发现估计是来自赫德岛的独立入侵,而不是澳大利亚大陆当地的 传播。没有证据表明与澳大利亚地方性 禽流感 病毒重配,并且两种病毒序列都保留了关键的类禽遗传标记,并且缺乏已知的降低 抗病毒药物 易感性的替代。这些检测揭示了 H5N1 反复侵入澳大利亚的南大洋途径,凸显了在大陆潜在定居的风险,以及加强 监测 和快速、全国协调的病毒基因组特征分析的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了尽管 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 在全球范围内传播的情况,但在澳大利亚大陆尚未确认检测到 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的问题,旨在确定入侵该大陆的来源和途径。
设计与对象
该研究报告首次在 2026 年 6 月在西澳大利亚南海岸发现的两只活海鸟(一只棕色贼鸥和一只南方巨海燕)中检测到 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b。
核心方法
进行病毒基因组测序以确定检测到的病毒的遗传关系,并进行时间校准的系统发育分析以推断最可能的源位置和入侵途径。
主要结果
在西澳大利亚检测到的两种病毒与来自南印度洋亚南极岛屿的 H5N1 病毒关系最密切,系统发育地理学分析确定赫德岛是最有可能的来源地点。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了 H5N1 反复入侵澳大利亚的南大洋途径,这是首次在大陆确认发现该分支,并强调了潜在定植的风险。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要加强 监测 和快速、全国协调的病毒基因组表征,以监测和减轻 H5N1 在澳大利亚传播的风险。
局限与证据强度
由于赫德岛和澳大利亚大陆之间缺乏中间采样地点,证据受到限制,因此未采样的 传播 路线存在不确定性,而且该研究依赖于两次检测的小样本量。

First detected incursions of avian influenza H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b into mainland Australia from the Southern Ocean

Authors: Matthew J. Neave, Sam Hair, Patrick Mileto, Jackie E. Mahar, Vittoria Stevens, Kelly Davies, Mark O’Dea, Sadia Iqbal, Jamie W. L. Ong, Andrew Hughes
Original abstract (English)
Abstract High pathogenicity avian influenza H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has caused a panzootic of devastating impact to poultry and wildlife globally. The Australian continent and broader Oceania until recently remained the last major region without confirmed detections. Here we report the first H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b detections from two live seabirds - a brown skua and a southern giant petrel - found on the south coast of Western Australia in June 2026. Virus genome sequencing showed that both viruses were most closely related to H5N1 viruses detected recently on sub-Antarctic islands in the Southern Indian Ocean. In time-calibrated phylogeographic analyses, both viruses sampled in Western Australia clustered with viruses from Heard Island, a sub-Antarctic external territory of Australia. Ancestral location reconstruction also identified Heard Island as the most probable source location, although unsampled intermediate locations cannot be excluded. The two Western Australian detections were estimated to be independent incursions from Heard Island, rather than local transmission on mainland Australia. There was no evidence of reassortment with endemic avian influenza viruses in Australia, and both virus sequences retained key avian-like genetic markers and lacked known substitutions for reduced antiviral susceptibility. These detections revealed a Southern Ocean pathway of recurrent H5N1 incursions into Australia, highlighting the risk of potential establishment on the mainland and the need for heightened surveillance and rapid, nationally-coordinated, virus genomic characterisation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the absence of confirmed detections of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b in mainland Australia despite its global panzootic spread, aiming to identify the source and pathway of incursions into the continent.
Design and population
The study reported the first detections of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b from two live seabirds—a brown skua and a southern giant petrel—found on the south coast of Western Australia in June 2026.
Core methods
Virus genome sequencing was performed to determine the genetic relationship of the detected viruses, and time-calibrated phylogeographic analyses were conducted to infer the most probable source location and incursion pathways.
Main results
The two viruses detected in Western Australia were most closely related to H5N1 viruses from sub-Antarctic islands in the Southern Indian Ocean, with phylogeographic analyses identifying Heard Island as the most probable source location.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings revealed a Southern Ocean pathway of recurrent H5N1 incursions into Australia, representing the first confirmed detections of this clade on the mainland and highlighting the risk of potential establishment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the need for heightened surveillance and rapid, nationally-coordinated virus genomic characterisation to monitor and mitigate the risk of H5N1 establishment in Australia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the absence of intermediate sampling locations between Heard Island and mainland Australia, leaving uncertainty about unsampled transmission routes, and the study relies on a small sample size of two detections.

超越进入:评估 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 在澳大利亚的内部传播风险

Beyond Entry: Evaluating the Internal Spread Risk of highly pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 in Australia
作者： Catarina do Carmo Norte dos Santos, Santos CdCNd
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 病毒 H5N1 目前正在全球范围内传播,大洋洲是唯一未受影响的大陆。然而,最近在亚南极赫德岛野生动物中检测到高致病性禽流感凸显了入侵澳大利亚大陆的风险不断增加。迁徙水禽和滨鸟被认为是 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的关键 媒介,将澳大利亚与全球 传播 网络连接起来。在这里,我们开发了一个基于代理的模型来预测 H5N1 在澳大利亚不同地区潜在入侵后的传播。我们的结果表明,入侵区域对 暴发 传播和持续时间有很大影响。H5N1 在澳大利亚的传播给家禽 暴发 和潜在的 跨物种溢出 进入野生动物种群带来了巨大风险,特别是在生态敏感的繁殖区。这些发现为告知国家准备情况、指导有针对性的 监测 以及加强 One Health 战略以减轻 HPAI 对澳大利亚的影响提供了重要见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 在澳大利亚的内部传播风险,因为该病毒目前正在全球传播,而大洋洲基本上未受影响。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种基于代理的模型来预测 H5N1 可能入侵澳大利亚不同地区后的传播,重点关注将澳大利亚与全球 传播 网络连接起来的迁徙水禽和滨鸟。
核心方法
开发了一个基于代理的模型来模拟 H5N1 的传播,其中纳入了澳大利亚各地区候鸟模式和潜在入侵点的数据。
主要结果
结果表明,入侵区域显着影响H5N1 暴发在澳大利亚境内的传播和持续时间。
解释与创新
作者解释说,该模型提供了对 H5N1 如何在内部传播的新颖理解,强调了入侵区域在确定 暴发 动态方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为国家备灾战略提供信息,指导有针对性的 监测 工作,并支持 One Health 方法来减轻 HPAI 对澳大利亚的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于一个模型,该模型在捕捉现实世界的复杂性方面可能存在局限性,但证据得到了模型设计和 H5N1 全球传播背景的支持。

Beyond Entry: Evaluating the Internal Spread Risk of highly pathogenic Avian Influenza (HPAI) H5N1 in Australia

Authors: Catarina do Carmo Norte dos Santos, Santos CdCNd
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) virus H5N1 is currently spreading worldwide, with Oceania remaining the only continent largely unaffected. However, recent detection of HPAI in wildlife on sub-Antarctic Heard Island highlights the increasing risk of incursion into mainland Australia. Migratory waterfowl and shorebirds, recognised as key vectors of avian influenza viruses (AIV), connect Australia with global transmission networks. Here, we developed an agent-based model to predict the spread of H5N1 following potential incursions across different regions of Australia. Our results suggest that the incursion region has a strong effect on outbreak spread and duration. The spread of H5N1 in Australia creates a substantial risk for poultry outbreaks and potential spillover into wildlife populations, particularly in ecologically sensitive breeding areas. These findings provide critical insights for informing national preparedness, guiding targeted surveillance, and strengthening One Health strategies to mitigate the impact of HPAI in Australia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the internal spread risk of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 in Australia, as the virus is currently spreading globally and Oceania remains largely unaffected.
Design and population
The study developed an agent-based model to predict the spread of H5N1 following potential incursions across different regions of Australia, focusing on migratory waterfowl and shorebirds that connect Australia with global transmission networks.
Core methods
An agent-based model was developed to simulate the spread of H5N1, incorporating data on migratory bird patterns and potential incursion points across various Australian regions.
Main results
The results indicate that the incursion region significantly affects the spread and duration of H5N1 outbreaks within Australia.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the model provides a novel understanding of how H5N1 could spread internally, emphasizing the importance of the incursion region in determining outbreak dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform national preparedness strategies, guide targeted surveillance efforts, and support One Health approaches to mitigate the impact of HPAI in Australia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a model, which may have limitations in capturing real-world complexities, but the evidence is supported by the model's design and the context of H5N1's global spread.

2014年至2025年中国H9N2 进化病毒的基因进化、系统动力学、地理传播:潜在人畜共患病风险不断增加。

Genetic evolution, phylodynamics, geographic spread of H9N2 avian influenza viruses in China from 2014 to 2025: an increasing potential zoonotic risk.
作者： Zifeng Pang, Peiting Zhong, Cuishan Mai, Junkun Fan, Yejia Wu, Yao Zhang, Hanlin Liu, Hailiang Sun, Ming Liao
摘要中文翻译禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的 H9N2 亚型对家禽业和公共卫生构成越来越大的威胁。但自2014年以来,国内尚未对其基因进化、基因型、空间动态及致病性进行系统研究。因此,我们利用公共数据库和实验室分离株对2014年至2025年中国H9N2病毒的基因组进行了大规模序列分析。在本研究中,我们在 1,591 种 H9N2 病毒中鉴定出 52 种不同的基因型,包括 4 种先前识别的基因型(G6、G57、G58 和 G68)和 48 种新定义的基因型(G118-G163)。G57和G118是2014-2025年中国主要流行基因型。贝叶斯谱系地理学分析显示,2017-2022年H9N2禽流感在中国传播存在12条明显的迁徙路径,其中华南地区是传播的主要中心。H9N2 AIV 继续在中国的鸡和鸭中传播,并传播到其他 宿主。具有G57和G118基因型的H9N2 AIV可以在MDCK、CEF、A549和HBE细胞中有效复制,滴度为0.97-8.5 lgTCID50/mL。G57和G118基因型H9N2病毒优先与α-2,6连接的唾液酸糖聚合物(人类受体)结合,并在小鼠和鸡的多个器官中有效复制并引起肺部病理变化。因此,我国有必要加强H9N2禽流感的监测和预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H9N2 进化 病毒 (AIV) 对家禽和公共卫生的威胁不断升级,但在 2014 年之前,尚未对中国的 进化 基因、基因型多样性、空间传播或致病性进行系统分析。
设计与对象
该研究利用公共数据库和实验室分离株,对 2014 年至 2025 年在中国传播的 H9N2 禽流感病毒进行了大规模基因组序列分析。
核心方法
检索并分析 基因组 序列,以定义基因型,并对 2017 年至 2022 年中国地区的病毒迁移模式进行贝叶斯系统发育地理学重建。
主要结果
在1,591个H9N2病毒中总共鉴定出52个基因型,其中包括48个新定义的基因型(G118-G163),其中G57和G118成为主要流行基因型。
解释与创新
作者将 H9N2 基因型的快速多样化和地理扩张解释为人畜共患潜力增加的证据,并强调 G57 和 G118 是特别值得关注的基因型。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持中国加强 H9N2 AIV 监测 和预防策略,以减少家禽损失并降低人畜共患 跨物种溢出 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于公开可用的序列和实验室分离株,这可能不足以代表地理和时间多样性,限制了普遍性并引入了抽样偏差。

Genetic evolution, phylodynamics, geographic spread of H9N2 avian influenza viruses in China from 2014 to 2025: an increasing potential zoonotic risk.

Authors: Zifeng Pang, Peiting Zhong, Cuishan Mai, Junkun Fan, Yejia Wu, Yao Zhang, Hanlin Liu, Hailiang Sun, Ming Liao
Original abstract (English)
Subtype H9N2 of the avian influenza virus (AIV) poses a growing threat to the poultry industry and public health. However, since 2014, there has been no systematic study of its genetic evolution, genotype, spatial dynamics, and pathogenicity in China. Therefore, we performed a large-scale sequence analysis of the genome of Chinese H9N2 viruses from 2014 to 2025 using public databases and laboratory isolates. We identified 52 different genotypes in 1,591 H9N2 viruses, including 4 previously recognized genotypes (G6, G57, G58, and G68) and 48 newly defined genotypes (G118-G163) in this study. G57 and G118 were the main epidemic genotypes in China from 2014 to 2025. Bayesian phylogeographic analysis showed that there were 12 obvious migration paths for the spread of H9N2 AIVs in China from 2017 to 2022. In particular, the South China region was the main transmission centre. H9N2 AIV continues to circulate in chickens and ducks in China and spreads to other hosts. The H9N2 AIVs with the G57 and G118 genotypes could effectively replicate in MDCK, CEF, A549, and HBE cells with titres of 0.97-8.5 lgTCID50/mL. The G57 and G118 genotypes H9N2 viruses preferentially bound to α-2,6-linked sialic acid glycopolymers (human receptors), and effectively replicate in multiple organs of mice and chickens and cause pathological changes in the lungs. Thus, it is necessary to strengthen the monitoring and prevention of H9N2 AIVs in China.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) is an escalating threat to poultry and public health, yet prior to 2014 no systematic analysis had characterized its genetic evolution, genotype diversity, spatial spread, or pathogenicity in China.
Design and population
The study conducted a large-scale genomic sequence analysis of H9N2 AIVs circulating in China from 2014 to 2025 using public databases and laboratory isolates.
Core methods
Genome sequences were retrieved and analyzed to define genotypes and perform Bayesian phylogeographic reconstruction of viral migration patterns across Chinese regions from 2017 to 2022.
Main results
A total of 52 genotypes were identified among 1,591 H9N2 viruses, including 48 newly defined genotypes (G118–G163), with G57 and G118 emerging as the dominant epidemic genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the rapid diversification and geographic expansion of H9N2 genotypes as evidence of increasing zoonotic potential, highlighting G57 and G118 as genotypes of particular concern.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support enhanced surveillance and prevention strategies for H9N2 AIV in China to mitigate poultry losses and reduce zoonotic spillover risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on publicly available sequences and laboratory isolates, which may underrepresent geographic and temporal diversity, limiting generalizability and introducing sampling bias.

2021-2022 年北卡罗来纳州牲畜生产强度以及对 H5N1 高致病性禽流感 A 病毒的粘膜 IgA 和 IgG 反应

Livestock production intensity and mucosal IgA and IgG responses to H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza A virus, North Carolina, 2021-2022
作者： Nora Pisanic, Kathleen M. Kurowski, Timothy Carter, Bonita D. Salmerón, Kristoffer Spicer, K. Krucynski, Carolyn Gigot, Laura Schmidt, Matthew A. Aubourg, Devon J. Hall
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人畜共患流感会带来公共卫生风险,直接接触牲畜是 H5N1 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A 病毒感染的已知危险因素。然而,目前尚不清楚生活在家禽和生猪生产强度较高的地区是否会增加暴露风险,而与职业接触无关。
设计与对象
这项横断面研究收集了 2021 年至 2022 年北卡罗来纳州工业畜牧业工人 (ILO-W)、其邻居 (ILO-N) 和大都市区家庭 (Metro) 的唾液样本,以评估粘膜甲型流感抗体反应。
核心方法
分析唾液样本中针对 H5N1、H1N1 和 H3N2 的粘膜甲型流感血凝素 (HA) IgA 和 IgG 抗体,并使用考虑参与者水平聚类的回归模型检查与家禽生产强度 (PPI)、猪生产强度 (SPI)、暴露组和猪特异性粪便污染标记 (Pig-2-Bac DNA) 之间的关联。
主要结果
居住在最高 PPI 和 SPI 三分位的人与显着较高的粘膜 H5 和 H1 HA IgA 水平相关,包括在没有职业性牲畜接触的居民中。职业性接触家禽或猪的家庭的 H5 IgA 和 IgG 以及 H1 IgA 水平显着高于都市家庭。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,证明居住地接触集约化畜牧生产和猪粪便污染的分子标记物与 H5 和 H1 甲型流感病毒粘膜抗体升高有关,这突显了职业接触之外的人畜共患接触的潜在途径。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究结果表明,监测 和 H5N1 HPAI 预防策略应将居住地邻近高密度家禽和猪养殖场以及环境污染视为风险因素,即使是在非职业暴露的社区成员中也是如此。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,排除了因果推断,并且依赖抗体检测而不是直接病毒检测,可能反映了过去的暴露而不是当前的感染风险。考虑到稳健的抽样和统计调整,证据强度中等,但受到设计限制。

Livestock production intensity and mucosal IgA and IgG responses to H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza A virus, North Carolina, 2021-2022

Authors: Nora Pisanic, Kathleen M. Kurowski, Timothy Carter, Bonita D. Salmerón, Kristoffer Spicer, K. Krucynski, Carolyn Gigot, Laura Schmidt, Matthew A. Aubourg, Devon J. Hall
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Direct livestock exposure is a risk factor for zoonotic influenza, including H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A virus. But whether living in regions of high poultry and swine production intensity (PPI, SPI) increases risk of exposure to zoonotic influenza viruses independent of occupational livestock contact remains unclear. Objectives To determine whether livestock workers and community members with no occupational livestock exposure in North Carolina, where poultry and swine production are increasingly co-located, are at higher risk of exposure to zoonotic influenza. Methods Saliva samples from industrial livestock operation worker (ILO-W), ILO neighbor (ILO-N) and metropolitan area (Metro) households were analyzed for mucosal influenza A (H5N1, H1N1, and H3N2) hemagglutinin (HA) IgA and IgG antibodies to determine associations of PPI, SPI, exposure group, and detection of a swine-specific fecal contamination marker (Pig-2-Bac DNA) with influenza A antibody levels. Results Residing in the highest PPI and SPI tertile was associated with significantly higher mucosal H5 and H1 HA IgA levels, including among residents without occupational livestock exposure. Households with occupational poultry or swine contact had significantly higher H5 IgA and IgG and H1 IgA levels compared to Metro households. In regression models accounting for clustering at the participant level, log 10 anti-H5 HA mucosal IgA increased 0.16 (95% CI: 0.06, 0.27, p <0.005) and 0.10 (95% CI: 0.03, 0.17, p <0.005), per log 10 increase in PPI and SPI, respectively, and 0.16 (95% CI: 0.03, 0.19, p <0.02) when Pig-2-Bac DNA was detected on household surfaces. Conclusions Mucosal H5 HA IgA and IgG and H1 HA IgA were consistently elevated across different metrics of livestock exposure intensity, including residential exposure, occupational contact within a household, and a molecular marker of household swine fecal contamination in a state with intensive poultry and swine production.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Zoonotic influenza poses a public-health risk, and direct livestock exposure is a known risk factor for H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A virus infection. However, it remains unclear whether living in regions with high poultry and swine production intensity increases exposure risk independent of occupational contact.
Design and population
This cross-sectional study collected saliva samples from industrial livestock operation workers (ILO-W), their neighbors (ILO-N), and metropolitan area households (Metro) in North Carolina during 2021–2022 to assess mucosal influenza A antibody responses.
Core methods
Saliva samples were analyzed for mucosal influenza A hemagglutinin (HA) IgA and IgG antibodies against H5N1, H1N1, and H3N2, and associations were examined with poultry production intensity (PPI), swine production intensity (SPI), exposure group, and a swine-specific fecal contamination marker (Pig-2-Bac DNA) using regression models accounting for participant-level clustering.
Main results
Residing in the highest PPI and SPI tertiles was associated with significantly higher mucosal H5 and H1 HA IgA levels, including among residents without occupational livestock exposure. Households with occupational poultry or swine contact had significantly higher H5 IgA and IgG and H1 IgA levels than Metro households.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that residential exposure to intensive livestock production and molecular markers of swine fecal contamination are associated with elevated mucosal antibodies to H5 and H1 influenza A viruses, highlighting a potential pathway for zoonotic exposure beyond occupational contact.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that surveillance and prevention strategies for H5N1 HPAI should consider residential proximity to high-density poultry and swine operations and environmental contamination as risk factors, even among non-occupationally exposed community members.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which precludes causal inference, and reliance on antibody detection rather than direct virus detection, potentially reflecting past exposure rather than current infection risk. The evidence strength is moderate given the robust sampling and statistical adjustment but constrained by design limitations.

分子 监测 和纳米孔测序揭示了乌克兰候鸟中低致病性 禽流感 A 病毒的循环和遗传多样性

Molecular Surveillance and Nanopore Sequencing Reveal the Circulation and Genetic Diversity of Low-Pathogenic Avian Influenza A Viruses in Migratory Birds from Ukraine
作者： Anton Gerilovych, Ivan Rusev, Ihor Korovin, Anastasiia Rusanova, Gregory Glass
摘要中文翻译野生候鸟是甲型流感病毒(IAV)的主要天然储存宿主,并在其进化和全球传播中发挥着核心作用。乌克兰位于黑海-地中海迁徙路线沿线,使得 野生鸟类 中的 监测 病毒在 One Health 框架内至关重要,尽管该地区的基因组数据仍然有限。2025年10月至2026年1月,有的地方说2025年12月,有的地方说2026年1月。我不明白其中的区别,从图兹利夫斯基国家自然公园及周边地区的候鸟和混种鸡群中采集了604份泄殖腔和口咽拭子以及环境粪便样本,以及附近家禽的样本。通过针对基质基因的实时 RT-PCR 检测甲型流感病毒,然后进行 H5/H7 筛选、完整血凝素基因的牛津纳米孔测序以及系统发育分析。在 30/604 个样本 (4.97%) 中鉴定出甲型流感病毒 RNA,而所有样本的 H5 和 H7 亚型均为阴性。完整的血凝素序列揭示了三种低致病性亚型(H1、H4 和 H6),它们聚集在已建立的欧亚谱系中,与欧洲、亚洲和北美流行的当代禽病毒密切相关。家禽中未检测到甲型流感病毒和新城疫病毒。这些发现为乌克兰的 禽流感 监测 提供了重要的基因组基线,并证明了便携式纳米孔测序和集成 One Health 监测 在监测病毒多样性和支持早期检测新出现的流感威胁方面的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
野生候鸟是甲型流感病毒的主要天然 储存宿主,并推动其在全球传播,但尽管乌克兰位于主要迁徙路线上,但来自乌克兰的 储存宿主 数据仍然有限。该研究旨在通过表征乌克兰境内候鸟中低致病性 禽流感 A 病毒的传播和遗传多样性来解决这一知识差距。
设计与对象
2025 年 10 月至 2026 年 1 月期间,在图兹利夫斯基利曼尼国家自然公园及周边地区(包括附近的家禽)进行了一项横断面 监测 研究。样本包括 604 份泄殖腔和口咽拭子以及从候鸟和混合物种鸡群收集的环境粪便样本。
核心方法
使用针对基质基因的实时 RT-PCR 进行甲型流感病毒检测,然后进行 H5/H7 亚型筛查。阳性样本接受了完整血凝素基因的牛津纳米孔测序,并进行系统发育分析以确定遗传关系。
主要结果
604 个样本中的 30 个(4.97%)检测到甲型流感病毒 RNA,而所有样本的 H5 和 H7 亚型检测均为阴性。完整的血凝素测序鉴定出三种低致病性亚型(H1、H4 和 H6),它们聚集在已建立的欧亚谱系中,与欧洲、亚洲和北美流行的当代禽病毒密切相关。
解释与创新
该研究证明了低致病性 禽流感 A 病毒在乌克兰候鸟中的传播,并为区域性 监测 提供了基因组基线。它强调了便携式纳米孔测序和综合 One Health 方法在监测病毒多样性和早期发现新出现的流感威胁方面的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为乌克兰的针对性 监测 战略提供了信息,并支持将基因组测序纳入国家 禽流感 监测计划,以加强早期预警和风险评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和有限的采样周期可能无法捕捉到病毒传播的季节性或地理变异性,并且未检测到 H5/H7 亚型并不排除它们在研究窗口之外的潜在存在。由于样本量较小并且依赖于单一地理位置,因此证据是中等的。

Molecular Surveillance and Nanopore Sequencing Reveal the Circulation and Genetic Diversity of Low-Pathogenic Avian Influenza A Viruses in Migratory Birds from Ukraine

Authors: Anton Gerilovych, Ivan Rusev, Ihor Korovin, Anastasiia Rusanova, Gregory Glass
Original abstract (English)
Wild migratory birds are the principal natural reservoir of influenza A viruses (IAVs) and play a central role in their evolution and global dissemination. Ukraine lies along the Black Sea–Mediterranean Flyway, making surveillance of avian influenza viruses in wild birds essential within a One Health framework, although genomic data from the region remain limited. Between October 2025 and January 2026 In some places you say December 2025 and in other places you say January 2026. I do not understand the differences, 604 cloacal and oropharyngeal swabs and environmental fecal samples were collected from migratory birds and mixed-species flocks in the Tuzlivski lymany National Nature Park and surrounding areas, together with samples from nearby domestic poultry. Influenza A virus was detected by real-time RT-PCR targeting the matrix gene, followed by H5/H7 screening, Oxford Nanopore sequencing of complete hemagglutinin genes, and phylogenetic analysis. Influenza A virus RNA was identified in 30/604 samples (4.97%), whereas all samples were negative for H5 and H7 subtypes. Complete hemagglutinin sequences revealed three low-pathogenic subtypes (H1, H4, and H6) that clustered within established Eurasian lineages closely related to contemporary avian viruses circulating across Europe, Asia, and North America. No influenza A virus or Newcastle disease virus was detected in domestic poultry. These findings provide an important genomic baseline for avian influenza surveillance in Ukraine and demonstrate the value of portable nanopore sequencing and integrated One Health surveillance for monitoring viral diversity and supporting early detection of emerging influenza threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Wild migratory birds are the principal natural reservoir of influenza A viruses and drive their global spread, yet genomic surveillance data from Ukraine remain limited despite its position along a major flyway. The study aimed to address this knowledge gap by characterizing the circulation and genetic diversity of low-pathogenic avian influenza A viruses in migratory birds within Ukraine.
Design and population
A cross-sectional surveillance study was conducted between October 2025 and January 2026 in the Tuzlivski lymany National Nature Park and surrounding areas, including nearby domestic poultry. Samples comprised 604 cloacal and oropharyngeal swabs and environmental fecal samples collected from migratory birds and mixed-species flocks.
Core methods
Influenza A virus detection was performed using real-time RT-PCR targeting the matrix gene, followed by H5/H7 subtype screening. Positive samples underwent Oxford Nanopore sequencing of complete hemagglutinin genes, and phylogenetic analysis was conducted to determine genetic relationships.
Main results
Influenza A virus RNA was detected in 30 of 604 samples (4.97%), while all samples tested negative for H5 and H7 subtypes. Complete hemagglutinin sequencing identified three low-pathogenic subtypes (H1, H4, and H6), which clustered within established Eurasian lineages closely related to contemporary avian viruses circulating across Europe, Asia, and North America.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates the circulation of low-pathogenic avian influenza A viruses in migratory birds in Ukraine and provides a genomic baseline for regional surveillance. It highlights the utility of portable nanopore sequencing and integrated One Health approaches for monitoring viral diversity and early detection of emerging influenza threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform targeted surveillance strategies in Ukraine and support the integration of genomic sequencing into national avian influenza monitoring programs to enhance early warning and risk assessment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and limited sampling period may not capture seasonal or geographic variability in viral circulation, and the absence of detected H5/H7 subtypes does not exclude their potential presence outside the study window. The evidence is moderate due to the small sample size and reliance on a single geographic location.

彻底改变应通报的禽类疾病诊断:使用 CENOS 平台验证 禽流感 和新城疫的直接拭子检测

Revolutionising notifiable avian disease diagnostics: validation of direct swab testing for Avian Influenza and Newcastle disease using the CENOS platform
作者： David EDGE, James TURTON, Adeola Adebo, Omeryasin Tuzaktepe, Brad Fraser, Craig S Ross, Joe James, Jacob TERREY, Nelson Nazareth, Scott Maclean Reid
摘要中文翻译现有的法定禽类疾病 (NAD) 分子诊断方法涉及一套 PCR 检测方法,可对 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 和新城疫病毒 (NDV) 进行一般检测和阳性亚型分析。使用未加工的泄殖腔 (C) 和口咽 (OP) 家禽拭子材料开发并评估了用于检测 AIV 和 NDV 的新型快速直接诊断方法。两种检测均采用闭管直接实时逆转录聚合酶链式反应 (RRT PCR) 方法,其中通过加热 治疗 和专用 PCR 兼容缓冲液实现病毒裂解,然后使用 RRT-PCR 方法进行检测。引物和探针组是使用过去五年收集的全球流通的 AIV 和 NDV 序列而不是区域特定序列数据集设计的,以便检测检测所有流通基因型。分析性能评估表明,两种检测方法均具有高度敏感性和特异性,可成功检测所有未提取的靶抗原,且不会与其他常见家禽病原体发生交叉反应。对于每次测定,病毒裂解和扩增都是直接从单位数 基因组 拷贝数的样品中实现的。此外,在存在 C 和 OP 基质材料的情况下,可以可靠地检测到低水平的病毒 RNA,为在现场直接检测这些具有经济意义的禽类病原体提供了概念证明。还探讨了其他用例场景,包括合并样本筛选和单个鸟类的组合 C/OP 测试。这些发现为正在进行的研究奠定了基础,该研究将配对样本测试与经过验证的实验室参考分析相结合。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对由 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 和新城疫病毒 (NDV) 引起的法定禽类疾病 (NAD) 进行快速、可现场部署的诊断的需求,这些疾病目前依赖于需要核酸提取的多步 PCR 测定。
设计与对象
该研究评估了使用未加工的泄殖腔和口咽家禽拭子材料检测 AIV 和 NDV 的新型快速诊断检测方法,无需提取核酸。
核心方法
两种检测均采用闭管直接实时逆转录 PCR (RRT-PCR) 方法,使用热 治疗 和 PCR 兼容缓冲液进行病毒裂解,然后使用 RRT-PCR 进行检测。引物和探针组是使用前五年全球流通的 AIV 和 NDV 序列设计的,以确保广泛的基因型覆盖。
主要结果
分析性能评估表明,两种检测方法均具有高度敏感性和特异性,可检测所有未提取的目标抗原,且不会与其他常见家禽病原体发生交叉反应。直接从单位 基因组 拷贝数的样品中实现病毒裂解和扩增,并且在泄殖腔和口咽基质材料存在的情况下可靠地检测到低水平的病毒 RNA。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为在现场直接检测具有经济意义的禽类病原体的概念证明,为正在进行的研究奠定了基础,通过与经过验证的实验室参考分析进行配对样本测试。新颖之处在于消除了核酸提取,同时保持了高灵敏度和特异性。
科研与公卫意义
这些检测可以使家禽中 AIV 和 NDV 的 监测 和 暴发 响应更快、更容易,从而减少与传统 PCR 工作流程相关的周转时间和后勤限制。
局限与证据强度
该研究提供了分析验证,但没有报告现场条件下的临床敏感性或特异性、汇总样本性能或组合测试结果,从而限制了对现实世界环境的普遍适用性。由于缺乏现场验证和样本多样性有限,证据强度中等。

Revolutionising notifiable avian disease diagnostics: validation of direct swab testing for Avian Influenza and Newcastle disease using the CENOS platform

Authors: David EDGE, James TURTON, Adeola Adebo, Omeryasin Tuzaktepe, Brad Fraser, Craig S Ross, Joe James, Jacob TERREY, Nelson Nazareth, Scott Maclean Reid
Original abstract (English)
Existing molecular diagnostic approaches for notifiable avian diseases (NADs) involve a suite of PCR assays that enable both generic detection, and where positive, subtyping of both avian influenza virus (AIV) and Newcastle disease virus (NDV). Novel rapid and direct diagnostic assays for the detection of AIV and NDV were developed and evaluated using unprocessed cloacal (C) and oropharyngeal (OP) poultry swab material. Both assays employ a closed tube direct real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RRT PCR) approach in which viral lysis is achieved by heat treatment and a dedicated PCR compatible buffer, followed by detection using a RRT-PCR approach. Primer and probe sets were designed using globally circulating AIV and NDV sequences collected over the preceding five years, rather than region specific sequence datasets, so that the assays detect all circulating genotypes. Analytical performance assessment demonstrated that both assays were highly sensitive and specific, successfully detecting all unextracted target antigens without cross reactivity to a panel of other common poultry pathogens. For each assay, viral lysis and amplification were achieved directly from samples at single digit genome copy numbers. Furthermore, low levels of viral RNA could be reliably detected in the presence of C and OP matrix material, providing proof of concept for direct detection of these economically significant avian pathogens in a field setting. Additional use case scenarios, including pooled sample screening and combined C/OP testing from individual birds, were also explored. These findings establish a foundation for ongoing studies incorporating paired sample testing against validated laboratory reference assays.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the need for rapid, field-deployable diagnostics for notifiable avian diseases (NADs) caused by avian influenza virus (AIV) and Newcastle disease virus (NDV), which currently rely on multi-step PCR assays requiring nucleic acid extraction.
Design and population
The study evaluated novel rapid diagnostic assays using unprocessed cloacal and oropharyngeal poultry swab material to detect AIV and NDV without nucleic acid extraction.
Core methods
Both assays employed a closed-tube direct real-time reverse transcription PCR (RRT-PCR) approach using heat treatment and a PCR-compatible buffer for viral lysis, followed by detection with RRT-PCR. Primer and probe sets were designed using globally circulating AIV and NDV sequences from the preceding five years to ensure broad genotype coverage.
Main results
Analytical performance assessment showed both assays were highly sensitive and specific, detecting all unextracted target antigens without cross-reactivity to other common poultry pathogens. Viral lysis and amplification were achieved directly from samples at single-digit genome copy numbers, and low levels of viral RNA were reliably detected in the presence of cloacal and oropharyngeal matrix material.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as proof of concept for direct detection of economically significant avian pathogens in field settings, establishing a foundation for ongoing studies with paired sample testing against validated laboratory reference assays. The novelty lies in eliminating nucleic acid extraction while maintaining high sensitivity and specificity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These assays could enable faster, more accessible surveillance and outbreak response for AIV and NDV in poultry, reducing turnaround times and logistical constraints associated with traditional PCR workflows.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides analytical validation but does not report clinical sensitivity or specificity in field conditions, pooled sample performance, or combined testing outcomes, limiting generalisability to real-world settings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of field validation and limited sample diversity.

开发基于多物种荧光素酶的双抗原 ELISA,用于检测甲型流感病毒 H5 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的抗体。

Development of a multi-species luciferase-based double-antigen ELISA for the detection of antibodies against influenza A virus H5 clade 2.3.4.4b.
作者： Andrea Aebischer, Anne Günther, Ronja Piesche, Kerstin Wernike, Magdalena Murr, Timm Harder, Donata Hoffmann, Christian Grund, Beatriz Bellido Martín, Ron Fouchier
摘要中文翻译未标记:H5N1 亚型的 高致病性禽流感 病毒对动物和公共卫生构成重大威胁。目前 H5 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的泛动物疫情已在各种家养和野生鸟类物种中造成大量、广泛的 暴发,导致高死亡率、大量损失,并且对意想不到的新型哺乳动物 宿主(例如奶牛)产生高频率的 跨物种溢出 事件。由于有效的哺乳动物间 传播,全球 H5N1 疫情引起了人们对人畜共患病风险的严重担忧。因此,增加扩大物种范围的 监测 强度至关重要,特别是在人-动物界面处。为此,迫切需要易于执行且适合高通量筛选的可靠且具有成本效益的血清学工具。新开发的双抗原酶联免疫吸附测定形式采用基于发光的检测技术,已证明符合这些先决条件。该测定可以在多种鸟类和哺乳动物(包括人类)中灵敏且特异地检测针对进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 H5 血凝素的抗体。此外,它允许区分 H5 抗头特异性抗体和交叉反应抗茎抗体,这对于使用 H5 特异性 疫苗 监控未来 疫苗接种 程序来说是一个有价值的功能。因此,该测定对现有的甲型流感病毒抗体的进化枝优化和物种独立检测的血清学诊断测试做出了重大贡献。重要性:正在进行的 高致病性禽流感 病毒 H5N1 泛动物疫情已在家养和野生动物中引起大量 暴发,频繁发生 跨物种溢出 事件,导致意想不到的 宿主 物种,这强调了加强 监测 的重要性。然而,敏感和特异的多物种血清学检测存在重大差距。为此,我们开发了一种新型双抗原酶联免疫吸附测定,采用创新的基于发光的读出策略。该测试可以对包括人类在内的多种鸟类和哺乳动物物种中的 H5 特异性抗体进行高度灵敏和特异性的检测。因此,它对改进用于检测甲型流感病毒抗体的独立于物种的血清学诊断工具做出了宝贵的贡献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H5N1 亚型的 高致病性禽流感 病毒对动物和公共卫生构成重大威胁。正在进行的高致病性禽流感病毒H5N1大流行已在家养和野生动物中引起大量暴发,频繁的跨物种溢出事件针对意想不到的宿主物种,这强调了加强监测的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种新型双抗原酶联免疫吸附测定,采用创新的基于发光的读出策略。该检测方法在包括人类在内的多种鸟类和哺乳动物物种中进行了测试,以确保其适用于不同的 宿主。
核心方法
新开发的双抗原酶联免疫吸附测定形式采用基于发光的检测技术,已证明符合这些先决条件。该测定可以在多种鸟类和哺乳动物(包括人类)中灵敏且特异地检测针对进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的 H5 血凝素的抗体。
主要结果
该测试可以对包括人类在内的多种鸟类和哺乳动物物种中的 H5 特异性抗体进行高度灵敏和特异性的检测。此外,它还可以区分 H5 抗头部特异性抗体和交叉反应抗茎抗体。
解释与创新
该测定对现有的血清学诊断测试做出了重大贡献,用于甲型流感病毒抗体的进化枝优化和物种独立检测。它代表了使用 H5 特定的 疫苗 监控未来 疫苗接种 程序的宝贵功能。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发改进的血清学诊断工具提供信息,以检测多种物种的甲型流感病毒抗体。这可以帮助 监测 人畜共患病风险和 疫苗接种 项目的监测。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。证据是强有力的,因为它表明该检测符合可靠且具有成本效益的血清学工具的先决条件。

Development of a multi-species luciferase-based double-antigen ELISA for the detection of antibodies against influenza A virus H5 clade 2.3.4.4b.

Authors: Andrea Aebischer, Anne Günther, Ronja Piesche, Kerstin Wernike, Magdalena Murr, Timm Harder, Donata Hoffmann, Christian Grund, Beatriz Bellido Martín, Ron Fouchier
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: The highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses of subtype H5N1 represent a major threat to animal and public health. The current panzootic with H5 clade 2.3.4.4b has caused numerous, widespread outbreaks in various domestic and wild avian species with high mortalities, massive losses, and a high frequency of spillover events to unexpected novel mammalian hosts, such as dairy cows. The global H5N1 situation raises serious concerns about zoonotic risks due to effective mammal-to-mammal transmission. Therefore, it is critical to increase surveillance intensity of a broadened species range, particularly at the human-animal interface. For this purpose, reliable and cost-effective serological tools that are easy to perform and suitable for high-throughput screening are critically needed. The newly developed double-antigen enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay format employing a luminescence-based detection technology has demonstrated compliance with such prerequisites. The assay allowed sensitive and specific detection of antibodies directed against H5 hemagglutinin of clade 2.3.4.4b in a wide range of birds and mammals, including humans. Furthermore, it allowed differentiating H5 anti-head-specific from cross-reacting anti-stalk antibodies, which represents a valuable feature with regard to the monitoring of future vaccination programs with H5-specific vaccines. Thus, the assay is a significant contribution to existing serological diagnostic tests for a clade-optimized and species-independent detection of influenza A virus antibodies. IMPORTANCE: The ongoing highly pathogenic avian influenza virus H5N1 panzootic has caused numerous outbreaks in domestic and wild animals, with frequent spillover events to unexpected host species, which underscores the importance of intensified surveillance. However, sensitive and specific multi-species serological assays represent a major gap. For this purpose, we developed a novel double-antigen enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay that employs an innovative luminescence-based readout strategy. The test allowed a highly sensitive and specific detection of H5-specific antibodies in a wide range of avian and mammalian species, including humans. It therefore represents a valuable contribution to improving species-independent serological diagnostic tools for the detection of influenza A virus antibodies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses of subtype H5N1 represent a major threat to animal and public health. The ongoing highly pathogenic avian influenza virus H5N1 panzootic has caused numerous outbreaks in domestic and wild animals, with frequent spillover events to unexpected host species, which underscores the importance of intensified surveillance.
Design and population
The study developed a novel double-antigen enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay that employs an innovative luminescence-based readout strategy. The assay was tested in a wide range of avian and mammalian species, including humans, to ensure its applicability across different hosts.
Core methods
The newly developed double-antigen enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay format employing a luminescence-based detection technology has demonstrated compliance with such prerequisites. The assay allowed sensitive and specific detection of antibodies directed against H5 hemagglutinin of clade 2.3.4.4b in a wide range of birds and mammals, including humans.
Main results
The test allowed a highly sensitive and specific detection of H5-specific antibodies in a wide range of avian and mammalian species, including humans. Furthermore, it allowed differentiating H5 anti-head-specific from cross-reacting anti-stalk antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The assay is a significant contribution to existing serological diagnostic tests for a clade-optimized and species-independent detection of influenza A virus antibodies. It represents a valuable feature with regard to the monitoring of future vaccination programs with H5-specific vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of improved serological diagnostic tools for the detection of influenza A virus antibodies across a broad range of species. This can aid in the surveillance of zoonotic risks and the monitoring of vaccination programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The evidence is strong as it demonstrates the assay's compliance with the prerequisites for reliable and cost-effective serological tools.

2025 年中国广东省家禽市场 禽流感 病毒横断面研究

A cross-sectional study of avian influenza virus in poultry markets of Guangdong China, 2025
作者： Wenwen Deng, Silin Xiang, Rongfei Che, Qianfang Guo, Shuling Li, Jiadian Cao, Zhe Liu, Yuxi Yan, Yuwei He, Chuting Zeng
摘要中文翻译背景 中国广东省是 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 出现的关键震中,H5N1 和高致病性 H7N9 都是在该地区首次发现的。监测该地区 AIV 基因型分布和遗传多样性对于提供早期预警和减轻人类感染风险至关重要。方法 2025年5月,对广东省4个代表性城市进行横断面研究。我们从 23 个活禽市场 (LPM) 收集了 500 个环境拭子和 45 个废水样本。对样本进行甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 筛查并进行 H5、H7 和 H9 亚型分析。采用下一代测序(NGS)和系统发育分析来表征病毒多样性和人畜共患潜力。结果 49.2% (246/500) 的环境样本中检测到 AIV。H9 是主要亚型(38.2%),其次是 H5(3.2%)和 H7(0.8%);2.4%的阳性样本表现出多亚型共现。“非限制区”市场(80.0%)和“垃圾存放区”(68.4%)被确定为主要污染热点地区。系统发育分析显示,H5 和 H9 病毒正在积累与增强的人型受体亲和力相关的 突变。值得注意的是,H5和H6序列表现出较长的内部分支,并与来自其他亚洲国家而不是国内菌株的分离株聚集在一起,突显了当前国内分子监测的显着差距以及这些基因型缺乏连续进化数据。结论 广东省低病毒感染地区持续存在广泛的禽流感病毒污染,以H9为主,高致病性亚型的低水平循环。已确定的 H5/H6 的 监测 差距和多亚型环境的存在强调了迫切需要加强、综合分子监测和更严格的市场监管,以减轻公共卫生风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
中国广东是 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 出现的公认热点地区,包括 H5N1 和高致病性 H7N9,这凸显了 监测 检测基因型转变和人畜共患风险的必要性。该研究旨在描述活禽市场中 AIV 基因型分布和遗传多样性的特征,为早期预警和风险缓解提供信息。
设计与对象
2025 年 5 月,我们对中国广东省的四个代表性城市进行了一项横断面研究。从23个活禽市场采集环境拭子(n=500)和废水样本(n=45),并对样本进行甲型流感病毒筛查,亚型为H5、H7和H9。
核心方法
对收集的样本进行甲型流感病毒检测和亚型分析,然后进行下一代测序和系统发育分析,以评估病毒多样性和人畜共患潜力。
主要结果
49.2% (246/500) 的环境样本中检测到 AIV,其中 H9 为主要亚型 (38.2%),其次是 H5 (3.2%) 和 H7 (0.8%);2.4%的阳性样本表现出多亚型共现。
解释与创新
系统发育分析表明,H5和H9病毒正在积累与增强的人型受体亲和力相关的突变,揭示了国内分子监测和H5/H6基因型的连续进化数据的差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了以H9为主的活禽市场持续存在禽流感病毒污染,高致病性亚型的低水平流通,强调需要加强分子监测和更严格的市场监管,以减轻公共卫生风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计和仅限于四个城市和 23 个市场的抽样限制了普遍性,并且对环境和废水样本的依赖可能不足以代表真正的病毒多样性;证据强度中等。

A cross-sectional study of avian influenza virus in poultry markets of Guangdong China, 2025

Authors: Wenwen Deng, Silin Xiang, Rongfei Che, Qianfang Guo, Shuling Li, Jiadian Cao, Zhe Liu, Yuxi Yan, Yuwei He, Chuting Zeng
Original abstract (English)
Background Guangdong, China is a critical epicenter for avian influenza virus (AIV) emergence, where H5N1 and highly pathogenic H7N9 were first identified. Monitoring AIV genotype distribution and genetic diversity in this region is essential for providing early warnings and mitigating the risk of human infections. Methods In May 2025, a cross-sectional study was conducted across 4 representative cities in Guangdong. We collected 500 environmental swabs and 45 wastewater samples from 23 live poultry markets (LPMs). Samples were screened for influenza A virus (IAV) and subtyped for H5, H7, and H9. Next generation sequencing (NGS) and phylogenetic analyses were employed to characterize viral diversity and zoonotic potential. Results AIV was detected in 49.2% (246/500) of environmental samples. H9 was the dominant subtype (38.2%), followed by H5 (3.2%) and H7 (0.8%); 2.4% of positive samples showed multi-subtype co-occurrence. “Non-restricted zone” markets (80.0%) and “waste storage areas” (68.4%) were identified as primary contamination hotspots. Phylogenetic analysis revealed that H5 and H9 viruses are accumulating mutations linked to enhanced human-type receptor affinity. Notably, H5 and H6 sequences exhibited long internal branches and clustered with isolates from other Asian countries rather than domestic strains, highlighting a significant gap in current domestic molecular surveillance and a lack of continuous evolutionary data for these genotypes. Conclusions Widespread AIV contamination persists in Guangdong LPMs, dominated by H9 with low-level circulation of highly pathogenic subtypes. The identified surveillance gaps for H5/H6 and the presence of multi-subtype environments underscore the urgent need for strengthened, integrated molecular monitoring and stricter market regulation to mitigate public health risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Guangdong, China is a recognized hotspot for avian influenza virus (AIV) emergence, including H5N1 and highly pathogenic H7N9, underscoring the need for surveillance to detect genotype shifts and zoonotic risk. The study aimed to characterize AIV genotype distribution and genetic diversity in live poultry markets to inform early warning and risk mitigation.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in May 2025 across four representative cities in Guangdong, China. Environmental swabs (n=500) and wastewater samples (n=45) were collected from 23 live poultry markets, and samples were screened for influenza A virus with subtyping for H5, H7, and H9.
Core methods
Influenza A virus detection and subtyping were performed on collected samples, followed by next-generation sequencing and phylogenetic analyses to assess viral diversity and zoonotic potential.
Main results
AIV was detected in 49.2% (246/500) of environmental samples, with H9 as the dominant subtype (38.2%), followed by H5 (3.2%) and H7 (0.8%); 2.4% of positive samples showed multi-subtype co-occurrence.
Interpretation and novelty
Phylogenetic analysis indicated that H5 and H9 viruses are accumulating mutations associated with enhanced human-type receptor affinity, revealing gaps in domestic molecular surveillance and continuous evolutionary data for H5/H6 genotypes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight persistent AIV contamination in live poultry markets dominated by H9, with low-level circulation of highly pathogenic subtypes, underscoring the need for strengthened molecular monitoring and stricter market regulation to mitigate public health risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s cross-sectional design and limited sampling to four cities and 23 markets constrain generalizability, and reliance on environmental and wastewater samples may underrepresent true viral diversity; evidence strength is moderate.

2014-2025年浙江西部外部环境H9N2亚型禽流感病毒分子流行病学特征

Molecular epidemiological characteristics of H9N2 subtype avian influenza virus in the external environment of western Zhejiang, China, 2014–2025
作者： Shiteng Huang, Bingdong Zhan, Min Wang, Lei Lyu, Jialing You, Ruijun Yang, Hui Yang
摘要中文翻译目的 揭示2014—2025年浙西地区外部环境禽流感病毒(AIV)流行病学分布规律,分析H9N2亚型分子流行病学特征,评估其公共卫生风险。方法根据浙江省职业暴露人群及外部环境禽流感规划,采用实时荧光定量RT-PCR技术检测环境标本中AIV亚型。将循环阈值<30的H9N2阳性样本接种到无特定病原体(SPF)的含胚鸡蛋中进行病毒分离,然后进行全基因组测序和生物信息学分析以进行系统发育和分子表征。结果 2014年至2025年共检测标本7,762份,AIV总体阳性率为34.64%(2,689/7,762)。季节、地区、采样地点和标本类型之间的阳性率存在显着差异(所有 p < 0.001)。禽流感病毒活动在冬季和春季达到高峰,活禽市场和案板拭子中检测到的感染率最高。H9 是主要亚型,多种亚型共同循环。所有48个H9N2亚型分离株均属于G57基因型,血凝素(HA)和神经氨酸酶(NA)基因属于Y280样分支,而内部基因则呈现出G1样和F/98样谱系相结合的镶嵌模式。分子特征分析揭示了多个哺乳动物适应性突变,涉及受体结合位点(T163N、H191N、T197D、T198V、Q234L、Q235M)、抗原表位(D280G、N285S)和糖基化位点(218NRTF、313NCSK)的改变。NA 茎缺失 (62–64 aa),以及血细胞吸附位点中的多个 突变(E/K368N、D369S/G、D401G/V、N402D、W403L/R、Q432H)。此外,还在内部蛋白质中发现了多个关键氨基酸取代。结论 浙西外部环境中AIV 患病率 较高,且时空聚集性明显。H9 是主要亚型,并与多种亚型共同传播,活禽市场和屠宰场被确定为高风险环境。H9N2亚型AIV积累了多个哺乳动物适应性突变,并在中国东部省份表现出遗传联系。这些发现共同强调需要一个综合的 One Health 监测 和预警系统来降低人类感染 禽流感 的风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了中国浙江西部地区 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 环境循环特征的公共卫生需求,该地区的亚型多样性和人畜共患病风险仍知之甚少。它的目的是绘制 AIV 分布模式,定义 H9N2 亚型的分子 流行病学,并评估相关的公共卫生风险。
设计与对象
2014年至2025年,浙江省监测职业暴露人群和外部环境禽流感项目在中国浙江西部开展了一项纵向环境监测研究。总共采集了 7,762 个环境样本并进行了 AIV 检测。
核心方法
使用实时RT-PCR检测环境样本中的AIV亚型。将循环阈值低于30的H9N2阳性样本接种到特定的无病原体的鸡胚中进行病毒分离,然后进行全基因组测序和生物信息学分析以进行系统发育和分子表征。
主要结果
在 7,762 份样本中,2,689 份(34.64%)AIV 检测呈阳性。阳性率因季节、地区、采样地点和标本类型的不同而存在显着差异(所有 p < 0.001)。禽流感病毒活动在冬季和春季达到高峰,其中活禽市场和案板拭子的感染率最高。所有 48 株 H9N2 分离株均属于 G57 基因型,HA 和 NA 基因聚集在 Y280 样分支中,内部基因呈现 G1 样和 F/98 样谱系的镶嵌模式。
解释与创新
作者将持续的高 患病率 和时空聚集解释为浙西 H9N2 持续环境循环的证据,其中活禽市场是主要的 储存宿主。他们强调哺乳动物适应性 突变 的积累和中国东部地区的遗传联系是潜在人畜共患病风险的新指标。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持在活禽市场和屠宰场等高风险环境中采取有针对性的环境 跨物种溢出 和基于风险的干预措施,以降低 跨物种溢出 风险。他们还证明加强 One Health 协调以进行早期预警和 暴发 准备是合理的。
局限与证据强度
该研究对环境采样的依赖可能不足以代表人类暴露途径,并且缺乏人类感染的直接证据。尽管进行了稳健的系统发育和分子分析,但实验 传播 或 发病机制 数据的缺乏限制了因果推断;总体证据强度被评估为中等。

Molecular epidemiological characteristics of H9N2 subtype avian influenza virus in the external environment of western Zhejiang, China, 2014–2025

Authors: Shiteng Huang, Bingdong Zhan, Min Wang, Lei Lyu, Jialing You, Ruijun Yang, Hui Yang
Original abstract (English)
Objective To elucidate the epidemiological distribution patterns of avian influenza virus (AIV) in the external environment of western Zhejiang from 2014 to 2025, analyze the molecular epidemiological characteristics of the H9N2 subtype, and assess its public health risks. Methods According to the Zhejiang Provincial Surveillance Program for Avian Influenza in Occupationally Exposed Populations and External Environments, real-time RT-PCR was used to detect AIV subtypes in environmental specimens. H9N2-positive samples with cycle threshold values <30 were inoculated into specific pathogen-free (SPF) embryonated chicken eggs for virus isolation, followed by whole-genome sequencing and bioinformatics analysis for phylogenetic and molecular characterization. Results A total of 7,762 specimens were tested from 2014 to 2025, with an overall positivity rate of 34.64% (2,689/7,762) for AIV. Significant differences in positivity rates were observed in seasons, regions, sampling sites, and specimen types (all p < 0.001). AIV activity peaked in winter and spring, with the highest rates detected in live poultry markets and chopping board swabs. The H9 was the predominant subtype, with co-circulation of multiple subtypes. All 48 H9N2 subtype isolates belonged to the G57 genotype, with the hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA) genes falling into the Y280-like branch, while the internal genes exhibited a mosaic pattern combining G1-like and F/98-like lineages. Molecular characterization analysis revealed multiple mammalian adaptive mutations, involving alterations in receptor-binding sites (T163N, H191N, T197D, T198V, Q234L, Q235M), antigenic epitopes (D280G, N285S), and glycosylation sites (218NRTF, 313NCSK). NA stalk deletion (62–64 aa), along with multiple mutations in the hemadsorption site (E/K368N, D369S/G, D401G/V, N402D, W403L/R, Q432H). Additionally, multiple key amino acid substitutions were also identified in the internal proteins. Conclusion The external environment in western Zhejiang exhibits a high prevalence of AIVs with pronounced spatiotemporal clustering. H9 was the dominant subtype and co-circulated with multiple subtypes, with live poultry markets and slaughterhouses identified as high-risk settings. The H9N2 subtype AIV has accumulated multiple mammalian adaptive mutations, and exhibits genetic linkages across eastern Chinese provinces. These findings collectively underscore the need for an integrated One Health surveillance and early-warning system to reduce the risk of human infections with avian influenza.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to characterize environmental circulation of avian influenza viruses (AIVs) in western Zhejiang, China, where subtype diversity and zoonotic risk remain insufficiently understood. It aimed to map AIV distribution patterns, define the molecular epidemiology of the H9N2 subtype, and evaluate associated public-health risks.
Design and population
A longitudinal environmental surveillance study was conducted from 2014 to 2025 in western Zhejiang, China, under the Zhejiang Provincial Surveillance Program for Avian Influenza in Occupationally Exposed Populations and External Environments. A total of 7,762 environmental specimens were collected and tested for AIV.
Core methods
Real-time RT-PCR was used to detect AIV subtypes in environmental specimens. H9N2-positive samples with cycle threshold values below 30 were inoculated into specific pathogen-free embryonated chicken eggs for virus isolation, followed by whole-genome sequencing and bioinformatics analysis for phylogenetic and molecular characterization.
Main results
Of 7,762 specimens, 2,689 (34.64%) tested positive for AIV. Positivity rates differed significantly by season, region, sampling site, and specimen type (all p < 0.001). AIV activity peaked in winter and spring, with the highest rates in live poultry markets and chopping board swabs. All 48 H9N2 isolates belonged to the G57 genotype, with HA and NA genes clustering in the Y280-like branch and internal genes showing a mosaic pattern of G1-like and F/98-like lineages.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the sustained high prevalence and spatiotemporal clustering as evidence of persistent environmental circulation of H9N2 in western Zhejiang, with live poultry markets acting as major reservoirs. They highlight the accumulation of mammalian adaptive mutations and genetic linkages across eastern China as novel indicators of potential zoonotic risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support targeted environmental surveillance and risk-based interventions in high-risk settings such as live poultry markets and slaughterhouses to reduce spillover risk. They also justify strengthening One Health coordination for early warning and outbreak preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on environmental sampling may underrepresent human exposure pathways and lacks direct evidence of human infection. Despite robust phylogenetic and molecular analyses, the absence of experimental transmission or pathogenesis data limits causal inference; overall evidence strength is assessed as moderate.

山猫 (Lynx rufus) 中 高致病性禽流感 A (H5N1) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的共同检测,华盛顿,2025 年

Co-detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1) and SARS-CoV-2 in a bobcat ( Lynx rufus ), Washington, 2025
作者： Hayley D. Yaglom, Zachary A. Barrand, Anna Schwabe, Katy L. Parise, Alysha Evans, Megan A. Dietz, Laura Williams, Beth Nielsen, Paige Hawkinson, Daryn E. Erickson
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 是重要的 One Health 病原体,其范围不断扩大。我们描述了对华盛顿州山猫 (Lynx rufus) 的一项调查,其中同时检测到 HPAI H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b(基因型 D1.1)和 SARS-CoV-2。临床症状、诊断结果和病理学与H5N1感染一致。本报告强调了综合野生动物 监测 对于表征人类与野生动物界面中新出现的人畜共患病原体的重要性。重要性 本报告记录了在野生动物康复机构收治的一只山猫身上同时检测到两种 One Health 威胁:禽流感 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2。虽然很少见,但同时检测到这些病毒凸显了哺乳动物常规监测中的差距,并说明了野生动物如何充当人与动物界面上病原体的桥梁 宿主。加强协调一致的 监测 工作对于了解疾病动态、保护动物和公共卫生以及支持更广泛的“同一个健康”准备策略至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查山猫体内 HPAI A(H5N1) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的共同检测,强调这些重要的 One Health 病原体不断扩大的 宿主 范围。
设计与对象
该研究涉及对华盛顿州一只山猫的调查,其中同时检测到 HPAI H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b(基因型 D1.1)和 SARS-CoV-2。
核心方法
该调查包括临床症状、诊断结果和病理学分析,以评估山猫是否存在 H5N1 感染。
主要结果
临床症状、诊断结果和病理学与H5N1感染一致,表明山猫体内同时检测到HPAI H5N1和SARS-CoV-2。
解释与创新
在山猫体内共同检测到这些病毒强调了整合 监测 的必要性,以了解人类与野生动物界面中新出现的人畜共患病原体。
科研与公卫意义
这一发现强调了监测哺乳动物是否存在禽源人畜共患感染的重要性,并说明了野生动物在人与动物界面上为病原体架起桥梁的潜在作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个山猫病例,这可能会限制普遍性,但由于联合检测的直接观察和一致的临床结果,证据是强有力的。

Co-detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1) and SARS-CoV-2 in a bobcat ( Lynx rufus ), Washington, 2025

Authors: Hayley D. Yaglom, Zachary A. Barrand, Anna Schwabe, Katy L. Parise, Alysha Evans, Megan A. Dietz, Laura Williams, Beth Nielsen, Paige Hawkinson, Daryn E. Erickson
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) are important One Health pathogens with expanding host ranges. We describe an investigation of a bobcat ( Lynx rufus ) in Washington State in which HPAI H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b (genotype D1.1) and SARS-CoV-2 were co-detected. Clinical signs, diagnostic findings, and pathology were consistent with H5N1 infection. This report highlights the importance of integrated wildlife surveillance to characterize emerging zoonotic pathogens at the human-wildlife interface. IMPORTANCE This report documents the co-detection of two One Health threats, avian influenza and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2, in a bobcat admitted to a wildlife rehabilitation facility. Although rare, the simultaneous detection of these viruses highlights gaps in routine monitoring of mammals and illustrates how wildlife may serve as bridging hosts for pathogens across the human-animal interface. Strengthening coordinated surveillance efforts is essential for understanding disease dynamics, protecting animal and public health, and supporting broader One Health preparedness strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the co-detection of HPAI A(H5N1) and SARS-CoV-2 in a bobcat, highlighting the expanding host ranges of these important One Health pathogens.
Design and population
The study involved an investigation of a bobcat in Washington State, where HPAI H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b (genotype D1.1) and SARS-CoV-2 were simultaneously detected.
Core methods
The investigation included clinical signs, diagnostic findings, and pathology analysis to assess the presence of H5N1 infection in the bobcat.
Main results
The clinical signs, diagnostic findings, and pathology were consistent with H5N1 infection, indicating the co-detection of HPAI H5N1 and SARS-CoV-2 in the bobcat.
Interpretation and novelty
The co-detection of these viruses in a bobcat underscores the need for integrated surveillance to understand emerging zoonotic pathogens at the human-wildlife interface.
Scientific and public-health significance
This finding highlights the importance of monitoring mammals for avian-origin zoonotic infections and illustrates the potential role of wildlife as bridging hosts for pathogens across the human-animal interface.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single bobcat case, which may limit generalizability, but the evidence is strong due to the direct observation of co-detection and consistent clinical findings.
